Script:
// For loop with standard semicolon syntax
sum = 0
for (i = 0; i < 5; i = i + 1)
    sum = sum + i
assert(sum == 10)

// For loop with multiple initializations using comma
product = 0
for (i = 1, j = 10; i <= 3; i = i + 1)
    product = i * j
assert(product == 30)

// For loop with multiple increments using comma
sum = 0
for (i = 0, j = 5; i < 3; i = i + 1, j = j - 1)
    sum = sum + i + j
assert(sum == 15)

// For loop with empty sections but semicolons present
count = 0
i = 0
for (; i < 3; i = i + 1)
    count = count + 1
assert(count == 3)

// For loop with empty condition section
count = 0
for (i = 0; ; i = i + 1)
    count = count + 1
    if (count >= 3)
        break
assert(count == 3)

// For loop with empty increment section
sum = 0
for (i = 0; i < 3; )
    sum = sum + i
    i = i + 1
assert(sum == 3)

// For loop with all sections empty
count = 0
for (;;)
    count = count + 1
    if (count >= 3)
        break
assert(count == 3)

// For loop with complex expressions
result = 0
add_one = fun(x) { return x + 1 }
for (i = add_one(0); i < add_one(2); i = add_one(i))
    result = result + i
assert(result == 3)

Script:
// For loop with standard semicolon syntax
sum = 0
for (i = 0; i < 5; i = i + 1)
    sum = sum + i
assert(sum == 10)

// For loop with multiple initializations using comma
product = 0
for (i = 1, j = 10; i <= 3; i = i + 1)
    product = i * j
assert(product == 30)

// For loop with multiple increments using comma
sum = 0
for (i = 0, j = 5; i < 3; i = i + 1, j = j - 1)
    sum = sum + i + j
assert(sum == 15)

// For loop with empty sections but semicolons present
count = 0
i = 0
for (; i < 3; i = i + 1)
    count = count + 1
assert(count == 3)

// For loop with empty condition section
count = 0
for (i = 0; ; i = i + 1)
    count = count + 1
    if (count >= 3)
        break
assert(count == 3)

// For loop with empty increment section
sum = 0
for (i = 0; i < 3; )
    sum = sum + i
    i = i + 1
assert(sum == 3)

// For loop with all sections empty
count = 0
for (;;)
    count = count + 1
    if (count >= 3)
        break
assert(count == 3)

// For loop with complex expressions
result = 0
add_one = fun(x) { return x + 1 }
for (i = add_one(0); i < add_one(2); i = add_one(i))
    result = result + i
assert(result == 3)
